== NOTICE

* `Exo 2:25` And God looked upon the children of Israel, and God took *_notice_* of them.
* `2Sa 3:36` And all the people took *_notice_* of it, and it pleased them, as whatever the king did pleased all the people.

_2 verses containing NOTICE_

== NOURISH

* `Gen 45:11` And there I will *_nourish_* you (for there are still five years of famine), lest you, your household, and all that you have should come to poverty."'
* `Gen 50:21` Now therefore, do not fear. I will *_nourish_* you and your little ones." And he comforted them and spoke kindly to them.
* `Isa 7:21` And it shall come to pass in that day, that a man shall *_nourish_* a young cow and two sheep.
* `Isa 23:4` Be ashamed, O Zidon, for the sea has spoken, even the strength of the sea, saying, "I do not travail nor bring forth children, neither do I *_nourish_* young men nor bring up virgins."

== NOURISHED

* `Gen 47:12` And Joseph *_nourished_* his father, his brothers, and all his father's household with bread, according to their families.
* `Deu 32:13` He made him ride on the high places of the earth. He ate the increase of the fields. He *_nourished_* him with honey out of the rock, and oil out of the flinty rock,
* `2Sa 12:3` But the poor man had nothing except one little ewe-lamb, which he had bought and *_nourished_*. And it grew up together with him and with his children. It fed from his own food, drank from his own cup, and lay in his bosom, and was like a daughter to him.
* `Sos 8:1` [Lady:] O that you were like my brother who was *_nourished_* at the breasts of my mother! When I should find you outside, I would kiss you. Indeed, I should not be despised.
* `Isa 1:2` Hear, O heavens, and give ear, O earth. For the LORD has spoken, "I have *_nourished_* and brought up children, and they have rebelled against me.
* `Ezk 19:2` And say, 'What is your mother? A lioness. She lay down among lions. She *_nourished_* her whelps among young lions.
* `Luk 11:27` And it came to pass, as he was speaking these things, a certain woman of the company lifted up her voice and said to him, "Blessed is the womb that bore you and the breasts which *_nourished_* you."
* `Luk 23:29` For behold, the days are coming in which they shall say, 'Blessed are the barren, the wombs that never bore, and the breasts which never *_nourished_* infants.'
* `Act 7:20` At that time, Moses was born and was exceedingly fair and *_nourished_* in his father's house for three months.
* `Act 7:21` And when he was cast out, Pharaoh's daughter took him up and *_nourished_* him as her own son.
* `Act 12:20` And Herod was highly displeased with those of Tyre and Sidon. But they came with one accord to him, and having made Blastus the king's chamberlain their friend, desired peace, because their country was *_nourished_* by the king's country.
* `1Ti 4:6` If you put the brothers in remembrance of these things, you will be a good minister of Jesus Christ, *_nourished_* by the words of faith and of good doctrine, to which you have attained.
* `Jms 5:5` You have lived luxuriously and in pleasure on the earth. You have *_nourished_* your hearts as in "a day of slaughter."
* `Rev 12:14` And two wings of a great eagle were given to the woman so that she might fly into the wilderness, into her place where she is *_nourished_* for a time, times, and half a time from the face of the serpent.

== NOURISHER

== NOURISHES

* `Isa 44:14` He hews down cedars for himself and takes the cypress and the oak, which he strengthens for himself among the trees of the forest. He plants an ash, and the rain *_nourishes_* it.
* `Eph 5:29` For no man ever yet hated his own flesh, but *_nourishes_* and cherishes it, even as the Lord the church.

== NOURISHING
